08       Canggang Railway Limited   • Annual Report 2023
                                                                       CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
                                                                               董事會主席報告




                                      Liu Yongliang
                                             劉永亮
                                           Chairman
                                         董事會主席




Dear Shareholders,                                                     各位股東：

On behalf of the Board, I hereby present the annual report of the      本人謹代表董事會呈列本集團截至2023年12月
Group for the year ended 31 December 2023.                             31日止年度的年度報告。

Overview                                                               概覽
The year of 2023 was an important year for the Group to start          2023年是本集團上市後開啟新征程、朝著規劃
a new journey after its Listing and stride towards its objectives.     目標闊步邁進的重要一年。一年來，公司管理
Throughout the year, the management of the Company adhered             層堅持以市場為導向，以效益為核心，以深化
to a market-oriented approach with efficiency as the core,             改革為手段，以創新發展為動力，圍繞市場開
deepening reforms as the means, and innovative development as          發、深化改革、戰略發展三條主線，堅持走出
the driving force. It adopted a three-pronged approach that centred    去，大興調查研究，大力實施「鐵路+」戰略，
around market development, deepening reforms, and strategic            積極開拓市場，狠抓安全管理，推行承包制改
development. It held fast to the principle of overseas expansion,      革，深入挖潛增效、積極推進項目建設，在艱
conducted extensive investigation and research, and vigorously         難的市場條件下保持了鐵路運量的基本穩定，
implemented the “Railway+” strategy. It actively expanded its          取得了比較穩定的經營業績。
markets, strictly monitored safety management, implemented
contracting system reforms, tapped into potential and enhanced
efficiency, and actively promoted project construction. As such,
a generally stable level of rail freight transportation volume was
maintained despite difficult market conditions, with which we
achieved relatively stable operating results.

STRATEGY
Since the Listing of our Shares on the Stock Exchange on 23          自 本 公 司 股 份 於2020年10月23日 在 聯 交 所 上

high standard of corporate governance. The Board believes that       治。董事會相信，良好的企業管治乃確保所有
good corporate governance is the basis in ensuring the proper        股東及其他持份者的利益皆得到妥善管理的
management in the interest of all our shareholders and other         基礎。完善企業管治是一項持久且系統性的工
stakeholders. Improvement of the corporate governance is a long      程，需要投入大量精力及毅力。鑒於營商及監
and systematic project, which requires strong commitment and         管環境不斷演變，並為符合持份者的期望，本
perseverance. As it always has, the Company will continuously        公司將一如既往持續完善風險評估和監察流程
improve the risk assessment and monitoring procedures, internal      及內部控制政策，並提升透明度及向股東作出
control policy, enhancing our transparency and disclosures           披露的質素，從而推動本公司可持續及茁壯發
quality to our shareholders to promote the sustainable and           展。
robust development of the Company in light of evolving business
and regulatory environment and to meet the expectations of
stakeholders.

development.

BOARD INDEPENDENCE EVALUATION                                            董事會獨立性評估
During the Reporting Period, the Company has established a board         於報告期內，本公司已制定董事會獨立性評估
independence evaluation mechanism which sets out the processes           機制，列明確保董事會具備充分獨立元素的流
and procedures to ensure a strong independent element on the             程及程序，令董事會得以有效行使獨立判斷，
Board, which allows the Board effectively exercises independent          從而更妥善保障股東權益。
judgment to better safeguard Shareholders’ interests.

The objectives of the evaluation are to improve Board effectiveness,     進 行 評 估 旨 在 提 高 董 事 會 效 率，盡 用 自 身 優
maximise strengths, and identify the areas that need improvement         勢，並識別需再作改進或進一步發展的範疇。
or further development. The evaluation process also clarifies what       評估過程亦可為本公司釐清具體行動以保持並
actions of the Company need to be taken to maintain and improve          提高董事會績效，例如滿足各位董事的個人培
the Board performance, for instance, addressing individual training      訓及發展需求。
and development needs of each Director.

Pursuant to the board independence evaluation mechanism, the             根據董事會獨立性評估機制，董事會對其獨立
board will conduct annual review on its independence. The board          性進行年度審閱。董事會獨立性評估報告會提
independence evaluation report will be presented to the Board            呈董事會，而董事會將集體討論評估結果及改
which will collectively discuss the results and the action plan for      進行動計劃（倘適用）。
improvement, if appropriate.

During the Reporting Period, all Directors has completed the             於報告期內，全體董事均以調查問卷方式獨自
independence evaluation in the form of a questionnaire individually.     完成獨立性評估。董事會獨立性評估報告已提
The Board independence evaluation report was presented to the            呈董事會，評估結果十分理想。
Board and the evaluation results were satisfactory.

of perspectives that are required to support the execution of the        衡。根據董事會多元化政策，本公司於甄選董
Company’s business strategy. Pursuant to the Board Diversity             事候選人時會考慮多項多元化因素，包括但不
Policy, selection of candidates for Directors will be based in a range   限於專業經驗、性別、年齡、文化、獨立性、
of diversity perspectives, including but not limited to professional     教育背景、知識、技能及服務年資。委任的最

background, knowledge, expertise and length of service. The              會作出的貢獻而作出。董事會相信，以用人唯
ultimate decision of the appointment will be based on merit and          才的準則委任董事將最能有利於本公司繼續為

CRISIS MANAGEMENT                                                         危機管理
The rail freight and road freight transportation businesses are           鐵路貨運及道路貨運業務本質上屬危險業務，
inherently dangerous and there are accidents which may result             會發生可能對我們的僱員和其他人士造成致命
in fatalities and other serious bodily harm to our employees and          傷害及其他嚴重人身傷害的事故。為堅守我們
others. To uphold our reputation of being one of the best company         作為當地最優秀公司之一的聲譽，並確保本公
locally and in order to help ensure that the Company is responsible       司有組織及有效地應付危機，本公司已成立保
to the crises in an organised and effective manner, the Company           安部管理危機。保安部由高級管理層組成，並
has established the security department to manage the crisis. The         由行政總裁密切監督。保安部的運作亦獲本公
security department comprises of the senior management and is             司資訊系統輔助，以記錄及追蹤最新的危機形
closely monitored by the Chief Executive Officer. The operation of        勢、相關事項和策略行動。保安部的職責包括
the security department is also aided by the Company’s information        以下各項：
system to keep track of the latest crisis situation, issues and
strategic actions. The duties of the security department include the
followings:

(i)    organize various safety activities and regular training sessions   (i)    舉辦各項安全活動及定期培訓課程，提高
       to enhance safety awareness and relevant work skills of our               員工的安全意識及相關工作技能；
       staff;

(ii)   strictly forbid staff who have not passed safety training and      (ii)   嚴禁未通過安全培訓及評估的員工參與貨
       assessment to work on our freight transportation;                         物運輸工作；

(iii) arrange inspection of our freight transportation monthly to allow   (iii) 安排每月檢視我們的貨物運輸工作，讓各
      each department to report any potential/existing deficiencies             個部門報告業務經營過程中的任何潛在╱
      during the operation of our business;                                     現有不足之處；

In order to manage the impacts on our businesses arising from the         為管理危機對業務造成的影響，本公司將設立
crisis, the Company will set up a safety production committee to          安全生產委員會全面負責安全生產管理，將保

BOARD STATEMENT                                                         董事會聲明
The Management Statement                                                管理聲明
This past year we’ve faced significant global challenges, including     在過去一年，我們面臨重大的全球挑戰，包括
economic instability, the ongoing impact of climate change, and         經濟不穩、氣候變化的持續影響，以及地緣政
geopolitical turmoil. Despite these hurdles and their ripple effects,   治動盪。縱使面對這些難關與隨之而來的漣漪
our commitment to promoting the sustainability of humanity and the      效應，我們致力推動人類及地球可持續發展之
planet has not wavered.                                                 承諾仍絲毫不變。

In light of mounting concerns regarding environmental, social and       鑒 於 對 環 境、社 會 及 管 治（「環 境、社 會 及 管
governance (“ESG”) issues and climate change, we acknowledge            治」）議 題 與 氣 候 變 化 之 關 注 與 日 俱 增，我 們
the critical importance of integrating ESG considerations to maintain   深知納入環境、社會及管治考慮因素，對維護
the Group’s reputation and ensure long-term viability. Therefore,       本集團聲譽及確保能夠長期發展至關重要。因
we continually strengthen our sustainability strategy, drawing on       此，我們不斷加強我們的可持續發展策略，集
our collective talents and expertise to foster a better community for   思廣益，發揮所長，為人們創造更美好的社區。
everyone.

The recent trials, particularly those presented by the pandemic,        最近的考驗，尤其是疫情帶來的考驗，突顯了
have highlighted the co-equal importance of the economy,                經濟、環境及社會三者均同等重要。我們明白
environment, and society. We understand that overlooking                忽略任何一個領域都會使我們難以長期成功。
any of these spheres can compromise our long-term success.              因此，我們非常重視三者相輔相成之關係。有
Consequently, we are mindful of their interdependence. In response,     見及此，我們將環境、社會及管治考慮納入決
we integrate ESG considerations into our decision-making process        策過程之中，同時致力秉承企業責任。隨著環
while striving to honor our corporate responsibilities. With ESG        境、社會及管治因素日漸影響企業發展，並涉
factors increasingly influencing corporate progression and involving    及 各 種 新 興 議 題，董 事 會（「董 事 會」）認 識 到
various emerging issues, our Board of Directors (the “Board”)           在企業發展當中更需要深思熟慮，並在監督環
acknowledges the need for careful deliberation in corporate             境、社會及管治事務方面承擔更大責任。我們
development and accepts a greater role in ESG oversight. We             已成立環境、社會及管治工作小組（「環境、社
have formed an ESG Working Group (“ESG Working Group”) to               會及管治工作小組」）以協調管治工作。小組團
coordinate our governance efforts. This team maintains close            隊與董事會緊密溝通，每年匯報同行的範例以
communication with the Board, annually presents peer examples for       作指引，持續關注並知會董事會有關環境、社
guidance, stays current with ESG market trends to inform the Board,     會及管治事宜的市場趨勢，以及確保本集團在
and ensures the Group remains updated on ESG initiatives. We have       環境、社會及管治計劃方面與時並進。我們已
established precise sustainability vision targets for the short and     制定精確的短期及長期可持續發展願景目標，
long term, committed to China’s goal of carbon neutrality by 2060.      致 力 於 在2060年 前 達 致 國 家 碳 中 和 的 目 標。

evolution of the regulatory, technological and market landscape due    技術及市場格局將因氣候變化而發生演變，包
to climate change, including the tightening of national policies and   括國家政策及上市規則收緊以及環境相關稅收

Reporting           A description of, or an explanation on, the application of   ABOUT THIS REPORT
Principles          the following Reporting Principles in the preparation of     關於本報告
報告原則                the ESG report:

                    Materiality: The ESG report should disclose:

                    (i)    the process to identify and the criteria for the
                           selection of material ESG factors;

                    (ii)   if a stakeholder engagement is conducted, a
                           description of significant stakeholders identified,
                           and the process and results of the issuer’s
                           stakeholder engagement.

                    Quantitative: Information on the standards,
                    methodologies, assumptions and/or calculation tools
                    used, and source of conversion factors used, for the
                    reporting of emissions/energy consumption (where
                    applicable) should be disclosed.

                    Consistency: The issuer should disclose in the ESG
                    report any changes to the methods or KPIs used,
                    or any other relevant factors affecting a meaningful
                    comparison.
                    描述或解釋在編製環境、社會及管治報告時如何應用下列報告
                    原則：

                    重要性：環境、社會及管治報告應披露：

                    (i)    識別重要環境、社會及管治因素的過程及選擇該等因
                           素的準則；

                    (ii)   如發行人已進行持份者參與，已識別的重要持份者的
                           描述及發行人持份者參與的過程及結果。

                    量 化：有 關 報 告 排 放 量 ╱ 能 源耗用（如適用）所用的標準、方
                    法、假設及╱或計算工具的資料，以及所使用的轉換因素的來
                    源應予以披露。

                    一致性：發行人應在環境、社會及管治報告中披露統計方法或
                    關鍵績效指標的任何變更或任何其他影響有意義比較的相關
                    因素。

Reporting           A narrative explaining the reporting boundaries of the       ABOUT THIS REPORT
Boundary            ESG report and describing the process used to identify       關於本報告
報告範圍                which entities or operations are included in the ESG
                    report. If there is a change in the scope, the issuer
                    should explain the difference and reason for the change.
                    解釋環境、社會及管治報告的報告範圍，及描述識別應納
                    入環境、社會及管治報告的實體或業務的過程。倘報告範
                    圍有所變更，發行人應解釋不同之處及變動原因。

environmental protection management system in our daily                系。我們的安全監控辦公室負責制定和實施有
operations. Our safety supervision office is responsible for           關環境保護的內部規則和法規。我們已在鐵路

of working procedure at various locations including railways and

laws and regulations.

determine those matters that were of most significance in the           事項對本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要，因
audit of the consolidated financial statements of the current           而構成關鍵審計事項。我們在核數師報告中描
period and are therefore the key audit matters. We describe             述這些事項，除非法律或法規不允許公開披露
these matters in our auditor’s report unless law or regulation          這些事項，或在極端罕見的情況下，如果合理
precludes public disclosure about the matter or when, in extremely      預期在我們報告中溝通某事項造成的負面後果
rare circumstances, we determine that a matter should not be            超過產生的公眾利益，我們決定不應在報告中
communicated in our report because the adverse consequences             溝通該事項。
of doing so would reasonably be expected to outweigh the public
interest benefits of such communication.

1 CORPORATE INFORMATION                                                  1 公司資料
     Canggang Railway Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated            滄 港 鐵 路 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為 於2018
     in the Cayman Islands on 19 October 2018 as an exempted              年10月19日 根 據 開 曼 群 島 公 司 法 第22章
     company with limited liability under the Companies Law,              （1961年第3號法例，經綜合及修訂）在開

      and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other

     (h) Leased assets                                                         (h) 租賃資產

           At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                本集團會在合約開始時評估有關合約
           the contract is, or contains, a lease. This is the case               是否屬於或包含租賃。倘合約給予為
           if the contract conveys the right to control the use of               換取代價而在一段時間內控制所識別
           an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for              資產的使用權利，則符合有關情況。
           consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer                 當客戶有權指示所識別資產的用途及
           has both the right to direct the use of the identified asset          自該用途獲得絕大部分經濟利益時，
           and to obtain substantially all of the economic benefits              即表示控制權已轉移。
           from that use.

              Significant increases in credit risk                                     信貸風險顯著增加

              When determining whether the credit risk of a financial                  於釐定金融工具的信貸風險自初
              instrument has increased significantly since initial                     始確認以來有否顯著增加，以及
              recognition and when measuring ECLs, the Group                           於計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團
              considers reasonable and supportable information                         會考慮與之相關的合理可靠資
              that is relevant and available without undue cost or                     料，且有關資料毋須付出過多成
              effort. This includes both quantitative and qualitative                  本或努力即可獲得。這包括基於
              information and analysis, based on the Group’s                           本集團的過往經驗及知情信貸評
              historical experience and informed credit assessment,                    估的定量及定性資料及分析，當
              that includes forward-looking information.                               中包含前瞻性資料。

              In particular, the following information is taken into                   具體而言，於評估信貸風險自初
              account when assessing whether credit risk has                           始確認以來有否顯著增加時會考
              increased significantly since initial recognition:                       慮以下資料：

              –   failure to make payments of principal or interest                    －     未能於合約到期日支付本
                  on their contractually due dates;                                          金或利息；

              –   an actual or expected significant deterioration in                   －     金融工具的外部或內部信
                  a financial instrument’s external or internal credit                       貸 評 級（倘 適 用）實 際 或 預
                  rating (if available);                                                     期明顯轉差；

              –   an actual or expected significant deterioration in                   －     債務人的經營業績實際或
                  the operating results of the debtor; and                                   預期明顯轉差；及

              –   existing or forecast changes in the technological,                   －     技 術、市 場、經 濟 或 法 律
                  market, economic or legal environment that have                            環境的現有變化或預期變
                  a significant adverse effect on the debtor’s ability                       化對債務人履行對本集團
                  to meet its obligation to the Group.                                       責任的能力造成重大不利
                                                                                             影響。

              Depending on the nature of the financial instruments,                    視乎金融工具的性質，對信貸風
              the assessment of a significant increase in credit                       險是否顯著增加的評估乃按個別
              risk is performed on either an individual basis or a                     基準或整體基準進行。倘按整體
              collective basis. When the assessment is performed                       基準進行評估，則根據共同信貸
              on a collective basis, the financial instruments are                     風險特徵（如逾期狀況及信貸風
              grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics,                     險評級）對金融工具進行分類。
              such as past due status and credit risk ratings.

              ECLs are remeasured at each reporting date to reflect                    預期信貸虧損於各報告日期重新
              changes in the financial instrument’s credit risk since                  計量，以反映金融工具的信貸風
              initial recognition. Any change in the ECL amount is                     險自初始確認以來的變化。預期
              recognized as an impairment gain or loss in profit or                    信貸虧損金額的任何變動均於損
              loss. The Group recognizes an impairment gain or                         益確認為減值收益或虧損。本集
              loss for all financial instruments with a corresponding                  團就所有金融工具確認減值收益
              adjustment to their carrying amount through a loss                       或虧損，並通過虧損撥備賬對賬
              allowance account.                                                       面值作出相應調整。

               of disposal. Value in use is based on the estimated                        成本兩者的較高者。使用價值乃

               amount.                                                                    減值虧損。

     (q) Income tax                                                        (q) 所得稅

           Income tax expense comprises current tax and deferred             所得稅開支包括即期稅項及遞延稅
           tax. It is recognised in profit or loss except to the extent      項。所得稅開支於損益內確認，除非

           income.

           to income taxes. It is measured using tax rates enacted           確定性。即期稅項根據於報告日期已

           also includes any tax arising from dividends.                     期稅項亦包括任何由股息產生的稅

           third parties. Revenue excludes value added tax or other                他銷售稅，並經扣除任何貿易折扣。
           sales taxes and is after deduction of any trade discounts.

           Where the contract contains a financing component which                 倘合約包含為客戶提供超過12個月重
           provides a significant financing benefit to the customer                大融資利益的融資部分，則收益按應

           present value of the amount receivable, discounted                      單獨融資交易中反映的貼現率貼現，

           interest method. Where the contract contains a financing                收益包括按實際利率法就合約負債所

        performance of, the Group’s various lines of business and
        geographical locations.

        Individually material operating segments are not                           個別重大的經營分部不會為財務報

        segments have similar economic characteristics and are                     似 經 濟 特 性，並 且 具 有 類 似 的 服 務

        class of customers, the methods used to provide the                        法，以及監管環境性質。個別非重大

     (a) Depreciation                                                       (a) 折舊

           Property, plant and equipment and right-of-use assets               物業、廠房及設備及使用權資產經計

           estimated useful lives, after taking into account the               按估計可使用年期折舊。本集團定期
           estimated residual values, if any. The Group reviews the            審 閱 物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 及 使 用 權 資
           estimated useful lives and residual values, if any, of the          產的估計可使用年期及剩餘價值（如
           property, plant and equipment and right-of-use assets               有），以釐定將於任何報告期內入賬
           regularly in order to determine the amount of depreciation          的折舊開支金額。可使用年期及剩餘
           expense to be recorded during any reporting period. The             價 值（如 有）乃 根 據 對 同 類 資 產 的 過
           determination of useful lives and residual values, if any,          往經驗並經計及預計該等資產未來如
           are based on the historical experience with similar assets          何配置的預期變動而釐定。倘與過往
           and taking into account anticipated changes on how such             估計有重大不同，未來期間的折舊開
           assets are to be deployed. The depreciation expense for             支會作調整。
           future periods is adjusted if there are significant changes
           from previous estimates.

     (b) Expected credit losses for trade receivables                       (b) 貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損

           The credit loss allowance for trade receivables are based           貿易應收款項的信貸虧損撥備乃基於
           on assumptions about the expected loss rates. The                   對預期虧損率的假設而定。於各報告
           Group uses judgement in making these assumptions and                期末，本集團會於作出該等假設及挑
           selecting the inputs to the impairment calculation, based           選輸入數據計算減值時，根據本集團
           on the Group’s past history, existing market conditions             的過往記錄、現時市場狀況及前瞻性
           as well as forward looking estimates at the end of                  估計作出判斷。該等假設及估計的變
           each reporting period. Changes in these assumptions                 動可能會對評估結果造成重大影響，
           and estimates could materially affect the result of the             且可能有必要於損益扣除額外減值。
           assessment and it may be necessary to make additional
           impairment charge to the profit or loss.

     (c) Net realizable value of inventories                                (c) 存貨的可變現淨值

           Net realizable value of inventories is the estimated selling        存貨的可變現淨值為於日常業務過程
           price in the ordinary course of business, less estimated            中的估計售價，並扣減估計完成成本
           costs to completion and selling expenses. These estimates           及出售開支。該等估計乃基於當前市
           are based on the current market condition and the                   況及出售性質類似之產品之過往經驗
           historical experience of selling products of similar nature.        而定，可因顧客偏好轉變及競爭者為
           It could change significantly as a result of changes in             應對嚴峻行業週期所作出的行動而大
           customer preferences and competitor actions in response             幅變動。管理層於各報告期末重新評
           to severe industry cycles. Management reassesses these              估該等估計。

           (iii) Statutory reserve                                             (iii) 法定儲備

                 In accordance with the relevant PRC laws and                        根據中國相關法律及法規，本公
                 regulations, the Company’s subsidiaries established                 司於中國內地成立及營運的附屬
                 and operated in Mainland China are required to                      公 司 須 將 有 關 公 司 的10%純 利
                 transfer 10% of their net profit to the statutory                   轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備結餘
                 reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of                    達 到 各 自 註 冊 資 本 的50%。向
                 the respective registered capital. The transfer to                  該儲備作出的轉撥須於向權益持
                 this reserve must be made before distributions to                   有人作出分派前作出。有關儲備
                 equity holders. This reserve can be utilized in setting             可用於抵銷累計虧損或附屬公司
                 off accumulated losses or increase capital of the                   增資，且除清盤外，不可用於分
                 subsidiaries and is non-distributable other than in                 派。
                 liquidation.

           (iv) Safety production fund reserve                                 (iv) 安全生產基金儲備

                 The Group is engaged in freight transportation                      本集團從事貨運業務。根據中國
                 business. In accordance with the regulations issued                 財政部及國家安全生產監督管
                 by Ministry of Finance and State Administration of                  理總局頒佈的規定，本集團須設
                 Work Safety of the PRC, the Group is required to                    立專項儲備（即安全生產基金儲
                 establish a special reserve namely safety production                備），有關儲備乃按上一個年度
                 fund reserve, which is calculated at 1% of the regular              一般貨運收益的1%及危險品貨
                 freight transportation revenue and 1.5% of the                      運收益的1.5%計算。
                 dangerous goods freight transportation revenue of the
                 previous year.

                 The safety production fund reserve is mainly used for               安全生產基金儲備主要用於購置
                 the acquisitions and maintenance of safety equipment                及維護安全設備及設施。就財務
                 and facilities. For the purpose of financial statements,            報表而言，有關儲備乃按上述方
                 such reserve is established through an appropriation                法透過自保留盈利撥款而設立，
                 from retained earnings based on the aforementioned                  並在產生相關開支時動用。
                 method and is utilized when the related expenditures
                 are incurred.

  (e) Capital management                                                  (e) 資本管理

        The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital                     本集團管理資本的主要目標為保障本
        are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a                    集團持續經營業務的能力，使其可透
        going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns                過使產品及服務的定價與風險水平一
        for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,                    致及按合理成本取得融資，以繼續為
        by pricing products and services commensurately with                     股東帶來回報並惠及其他持份者。
        the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
        reasonable cost.

        The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages                     本集團積極及定期檢討及管理其資本
        its capital structure to maintain a balance between the                  架構，以在較高股東回報（可能伴隨
        higher shareholders returns that might be possible with                  較高借貸水平）與穩健的資本狀況所
        higher levels of borrowings and the advantages and                       帶來的優勢及保障之間取得平衡，並
        security afforded by a sound capital position, and makes                 依照經濟狀況的變化對資本架構作出
        adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in              調整。
        economic conditions.

        Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are subject              本公司或其任何附屬公司概無受任何
        to any externally imposed capital requirements.                          外部施加的資本規定規限。

   INSTRUMENTS
     Exposure to credit, liquidity and interest rate risks arises in the      本集團在日常業務過程中面對信貸、流動
     normal course of the Group’s business.                                   資金及利率風險。

     The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk               本集團所面對的該等風險及本集團用以管
     management policies and practices used by the Group to                   理該等風險的金融風險管理政策和慣例載
     manage these risks are described below.                                  述如下。

     (a) Credit risk                                                          (a) 信貸風險

           Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default          信貸風險指對手方不履行合約責任導
           on its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to          致本集團蒙受財務損失的風險。本集
           the Group. The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable             團的信貸風險主要來自貿易應收款
           to trade receivables. The Group’s exposure to credit                     項。本集團就現金及現金等值項目和
           risk arising from cash and cash equivalents and bills                    應收票據所承受的信貸風險有限，原
           receivable is limited because the counterparties are banks               因是對手方為本集團管理層給予高信
           and financial institutions with high credit ratings assigned             貸評級的銀行及金融機構，故本集團
           by the management of the Group, for which the Group                      認為其信貸風險偏低。
           considers to have low credit risk.

           The Group does not provide any guarantees which would                    本集團不會提供將令本集團面對信貸
           expose the Group to credit risk.                                         風險的任何擔保。

           Trade receivables                                                        貿易應收款項

           The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by              本集團面對的信貸風險主要受各客戶
           the individual characteristics of each customer rather than              （而非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）的
           the industry or country in which the customers operate                   個別情況所影響，因此，重大集中信
           and therefore significant concentrations of credit risk                  貸風險主要於本集團與個別客戶有重
           primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure to               大業務往來時產生。於報告期末，貿
           individual customers. At the end of the reporting period,                易 應 收 款 項 總 額 中 的5%（2022年：
           5% (2022: 7%) of the total trade receivables was due from                7%）乃來自本集團的最大客戶，而貿
           the Group’s largest customer and 20% (2022: 20%) of                      易應收款項總額中的20%（2022年：
           the total trade receivables was due from the five largest                20%）乃來自五大客戶。
           customers.

      as pertaining to the economic environment in which the            特定資料。貿易應收款項於開票日期

      obtain collateral from customers.

      and contract assets at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs,          金額計量貿易應收款項及合約資產虧

      Group’s historical credit loss experience does not indicate       算得出。由於本集團過往信貸虧損經
      significantly different loss patterns for different customer      驗並無顯示不同客戶分部的虧損模式

      is not further distinguished between the Group’s different        提虧損撥備時並無在本集團不同客戶
      customer bases.                                                   群之間作進一步區分。
